# Epic Mystic Enchants: history, logic and testing

This file is generated by `scripts/legendary_history.py --quality Epic`; edit `enchants/epic-notes.csv` (assumptions,
sources, decisions, checked verdicts) or the test results, then run it again. The table of every epic copy (and the
lower-quality copies of the same enchants) is `enchants/epic-history.csv`. Legendaries have their own files
(`enchants/legendary-notes.md`).

## How to read an entry

- **Status**: `changed, awaiting in-game test` means a fix is merged but nobody has reported testing it.
  `tested (see test notes)` means a result is recorded; read it, because a recorded result can be a failure.
  `works from its data` and `handled by the core` mean we checked it and changed nothing. `unchanged: ...` is the
  tracker's audit verdict for a copy nobody has touched.
- **Changes table**: every commit whose added lines mention the enchant's spell id, the pull request that merged it,
  the first published server build that contains it, and the date patch night installed that build on our server.
  A commit can mention an id without changing that enchant (a shared list, for example), and a fix can reach an
  enchant through a different spell id (its proc, summon or transformed spell), so read the logic and pull request
  notes below as well.
- **How a change reaches players**: SQL in `sql/enchants/` is applied by patch night straight from GitHub, so an
  SQL-only change can be live before its build. Anything in `patches/` (the enchant scripts included) needs a server
  build, and is live only once patch night has installed that build.
- **What the server does**: copied from the comments in the enchant scripts and the SQL file headers, which describe
  each enchant's logic in plain English. **What the pull requests said** quotes the pull request descriptions, where
  most assumptions and guesses were written down at the time.
- **Assumptions**: anything we decided without a source (a number, a radius, how two effects interact).
- **Testing**: in-game results from `enchants/test-checklist.csv` and the tracker's tested column, with dates.
  "Nothing recorded yet" is the default; please add results there.

## Sources

1. **Ascension builder tooltips**: the archived Ascension builder (area-52 realm, snapshot of 14 June 2026). The text
   is Ascension's, so it is not kept in this repository; a private copy is on the game server
   (`C:\Games\CoA Server\ascension-mystic-enchants.json`).
2. **Our client's spell data**: Spell.dbc in the client's patches (effects, numbers and the enchant's own text). Where
   the builder has no entry (older or removed enchants), this is the only record.
3. **Core class versions**: several enchants copy a class ability that the CoA core already scripts (Blooming Shot,
   Eternity, Nature's Power); their numbers and behaviour were taken from those.
4. **Player descriptions**: written descriptions from players who used the enchant on Ascension, passed on by James
   in the development chat (Mana Fiend, Steward of Eternity, Grove Ranger's Blooming Shot).
5. **Public pages**: the Project Ascension wiki (Enchant Collection page) and the Ascension forum. Ascension's own
   database (db.ascension.gg) blocks automated reading and its news posts need a browser, so they were not used.
6. **Decisions**: calls James made where the sources disagreed or said nothing (listed under each enchant).

## Process

1. Open epics come from `enchants/tracker.csv` (status broken, partly working or missing, and no fix mentioning them
   yet), sorted by how much work they need: plain hooks and procs first, new spells and transforms last.
2. Fixes go in batches, each its own pull request, starting with epic batch 1 (#178). Every batch is compile-checked
   against the core release the build uses (also with the Windows header macros defined) and its SQL loaded twice
   into a scratch database. None of that replaces in-game testing, which is recorded per enchant below.

## Known core quirk

The core calls the heal hook (`UnitScript::ModifyHealReceived`) with its two units in a different order for direct
heals (healer first) than for heal-over-time ticks (target first). Batch 5 (`sql/enchants/051-epics-5.sql`) added
`WcmeHealParties` to the enchant scripts to put them in the right order, and fixed the three rules that had read them
the wrong way round: Growing Light (270661), Holy Ground (81315 / 281788) and the Align heal bonus (986160). Any new
heal rule should call `WcmeHealParties` first.

## Tooltip differences to review

Enchants whose in-game tooltip says something different from what the server does. Each was a deliberate
call (see its Decisions and Assumptions); check them again at every review, and fix the tooltip in the client
patch or the mechanic when there is a chance.

- Arngrim's Greeting (997188): Blizzard's text formula (520) doesn't match its data (645 over 6 sec).
- Blessed by the Elune (272253): Moonfire also deals its direct damage, which the tooltip doesn't mention.
- Blood Shield (288804): The shield caps (20% of maximum health, 10% against players) are our numbers; the tooltip's are blank in the data.
- Chilling Blows (81213): The tooltip can be read as 10.5 sec per Frostbrand hit on a stun-immune target; the server gives it once per Deep Freeze.
- Eclipse Bomber (275014): The Bomber buffs' own tooltips name the opposite Flames (data) from the enchant text; the server follows the enchant text.
- Elemental Lord (276578): The tethered-elemental parts of Elemental Aftershock and Elemental Lava are not done.
- Flowing (273418): Flowing's buff text says 2 stacks for Overflowing; the enchant's text and the script use 3.
- Necromancer's Touch (275963): Necromancer's Touch's Drain Life / Drain Soul stacks aren't used up by the next cast (its data has no charges), though the text says 'next'.
- Poison Bomb (979113): Its helper spell 979165 says 2 sec; the enchant tooltip and the server use 4 sec.
- Saber Fangs (984696): Saber Fangs' tick amount comes from the text formula; the data has none.
- Shadow Funnel (1180900): Void Nova deals Physical damage (its data) though the text says Shadow.
- Soul Restoration (282213): Its tooltip says Weakened Soul is shortened by 4 sec; the server uses 2 sec so every copy matches (James, 17 Nov).
- Spellstorm (271709): Arcane Evoked and Fire Evoked are an extra 80% roll of their own, used up by the next eligible spell, rather than a change to the talent's chance.
- Sweeping Swings (912326): Its helper spell 912327 says 2 sec; the enchant tooltip and the server use 1 sec.
- Unbounded Mind Flay (81370): Unbounded Mind Flay's damage is fixed at the rank 1 value (28 a second plus spell power) and doesn't follow your Mind Flay rank.
- Weapon Command (283338): The Ascension builder says +10% attack speed; our client tooltip and the server use +10% Physical damage.
- Weapon Command (954057): The Ascension builder says +10% attack speed; our client tooltip and the server use +10% Physical damage.

### Adrenaline Junkie (1589466, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 07911c5 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Finishing-move procs, 20% chance per combo point spent (10% for none here), checked at the cast while the combo points are still on you: - Cobra Combo (979106 / 284148): your pet gets 979105 (+15% critical strike chance). - Double Team (275094): 275096 on you (your pet's special attacks hit harder; its own data). - Deadly Combo (977856): needs Sudden Death; triggers Sudden Death (52437) and Deadly Combo (954353) through 977858. 283833: Sudden Death only (283835), and needs Sudden Death unless you have 973827. - Enraging Finale (283405): Bloodrage, if you know it (always with 973827), at most once every 20 sec and not while Bloodrage is up. - Titanic Finale (954436): the same Bloodrage proc from Rogue finishers while you can use Titan's Grip. - Adrenaline Junkie (1589466): 12 sec off Adrenaline Rush (its 1589467 value). - Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110): damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give a stack of Bloodsail Swashbuckling (983112, up to 10, 18 sec, the duration is not refreshed by new stacks) and the same number of stacks of 983113 (each main-hand auto attack gives 1 rage per stack, spell_wcme_bloodsail_rage). 285111: damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give 285115 (crit, up to 5). - Poison Bomb (979113): Envenom and Eviscerate take 4 sec off Poison Bomb (the text's 4 sec; its helper 979165 says 2). - Deadly Follow-up (979080): damaging finishers, 10% per combo point (the builder's number), give a charge of Poisoned Shiv (979082, up to 3), which Deadly Follow-up (979081) needs and spends. - Trauma Care (274205): Trauma Care (274211: next Greater Heal instant and +100%, its data), holding up to 2 charges; a new one starts with 1. - Party Recuperation (983475): 10% per combo point, a stack of Bubbling Tankard (983476, up to 4).
- 048-epics-2.sql: Finishing-move procs (spell_wcme_finisher_combo, 20% per combo point): Cobra Combo (979106, 284148), Double Team (275094), Deadly Combo (977856, 283833), Enraging Finale (283405), Titanic Finale (954436), Adrenaline Junkie (1589466), Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110, 285111; 983113 gives its rage on main-hand hits).

**Assumptions:** 12 sec (1589467's value) taken off any Adrenaline Rush you know. The GCD removal was already its data.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

### Adumbral Companion (81422, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 07911c5 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Companions (81418 Arcane, 81419 Fire, 81420 Frost, 81421 Nature, 81422 Shadow): your hunter pet's auto attacks deal only that school (the enchant's own school value) and 10% less damage (wcme_epic_batch2_hooks); the pet shows the Companion's visual (81424-81428). Kept in step every second; the pet's school is put back when the enchant goes.
- Epic batch 2 hooks: - I Have The High Ground (81090): 3% more damage when you are more than 5 yards above your target. - Companions (81418-81422): your hunter pet's auto attacks deal 10% less while one is on (their own -10). - Titanic Finale (954436): while the Enrage talent's buff is on you, you also have 954437 (+5% melee attack speed), for as long as Enrage lasts.
- 048-epics-2.sql: Weapon Command (954057, 283338: single two-handed weapon), the Companions (81418-81422: hunter pet auto attack school) and, as a hook only, I Have The High Ground (81090) and Titanic Finale's Enrage speed.

**Assumptions:** As 81418 (Shadow).

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

### Aegis Overdrive (299160, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 44ee48c | not merged yet | none yet | - |
| 2026-11-17 | 7910784 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Aegis Overdrive (299160): dodging, parrying or blocking with Mana Shield up has a 20% chance (its helper 299162) to set off Aegis Explosion (299163), at most once every 5 sec. The explosions on Mana Shield going up and ending are in wcme_epic_batch67_hooks.
- Epic batches 6 and 7, unit side: - Double Agent (81439): Ambush deals double damage to a target that was stealthed when the Ambush started (the core takes the target's stealth off before the damage is worked out). Its stealth detection is its data. - Physics in Practice (277460): Deadly Throw and Shuriken Toss deal more damage by your active Sprint's speed bonus (15% of it against players). - Shadow Word: Gore (983020): its ticks get the bonus worked out when it was cast. - Energy Surge (1180750): your damaging Rogue ability that hits a target with your Gouge deals 30% more (its own value); the Gouge then breaks as usual. - Ol' Bloodsurge (983600): using Bloodsurge makes your next Hunter or Warrior direct damage ability deal 20% more (within 15 sec). - Consumed By Shadows (997217): with Master of Subtlety (31665) up, your Shadow damage gets its bonus too (its value, 12% at rank 3); leaving Stealth puts you in Shadowform if you know it, unless you left Stealth by shifting into another form, are mounted, or are stealthed again. - Necromancer's Touch (275963): melee and spell hits from your Dominate Undead or Enslave Demon pet, or your ghoul, add a stack of Necromancer's Touch (275966) and of its Grave Coil part (288824) from you on the target (see NecromancerPet). Your next Grave Coil on the target uses up the Grave Coil stacks. - Searing Flames (1180640): each Immolate tick also hits every other enemy within 8 yards of the target for the same damage (1180643), and adds a stack of 1180644 (+3% spell crit) to your pet. - Lingering Death (81506): heals on the target take off what Shadow Word: Death dealt, until used up or 3 sec pass. - Downpour (983523): teaches Downpour (983524, wcme_enchant_teach). Each injured ally it heals gives you a stack of Torrential Riptide (983519, up to 5); when Riptide uses them, Downpour's cooldown drops 3 sec per stack. - Photosynthesis (287852): while your Lifebloom is on an ally (not you), each of your Nature heal-over-time ticks on them has a 20% chance to make it bloom (33778, worked out as the core's Lifebloom does) without using it up. - Vampirism (277003): Vampiric Embrace no longer heals you or your party; the part it would have healed you for goes instead to the lowest-health ally within 40 yards (you included), with your healing power added, up to 5% of your maximum health per heal. - Ursoc's Blessing (81446 / 282004): leaving Bear or Dire Bear Form keeps the armour and Stamina parts of the form's passive (Bear Form (Passive) / Dire Bear Form (Passive), effects 0 and 1, not its attack power) on you for 3 more sec. - Shadow Funnel (1180900): while you channel Health Funnel, each melee hit from your Voidwalker sets off Void Nova (1180902) around it. The no-threat part is its data. - Aegis Overdrive (299160): Mana Shield going up sets off Aegis Explosion (299163) unless its activation lockout (299161) is on you; Mana Shield running out or breaking sets it off again. - Trauma Care (274205) / Party Recuperation (983475): finisher procs in spell_wcme_finisher_combo.
- 053-epics-7.sql: With rows: Party Recuperation (983475: finisher proc, 10% per combo point), Twisted Mind (979779: Fel Armor shows as Twisted Mind), "Stealth" (982477, You're a Solid Crate: its stealth and slow helpers, crate removed after), Energy Surge (1180750: energy while a monster is Gouged), Aegis Overdrive (299160: dodge, parry or block with Mana Shield up).

**Assumptions:** Mana-Forged Barrier isn't checked. The activation lockout is its own helper 299161. 20% chance and 5 sec cooldown on avoidance from the text.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

### Arngrim's Greeting (997188, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 44ee48c | not merged yet | none yet | - |
| 2026-11-17 | 9298c60 | not merged yet | none yet | - |
| 2026-11-17 | f70bb96 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batches 6 and 7, spell side. Not here because the core already does them (review 2026-11-17): Electrical Discharge (277082, hard-coded in AuraEffect::HandleAscensionPercentEventProc), Pious Strikes (1581647) and Holy Servitude (1584997) (both coa_mystic_enchant_proc rules run by spell_ascension_mystic_enchant_proc). - Trauma Care (274205): a Greater Heal cast with Trauma Care (274211) also heals the lowest-health injured ally within 40 yards for the same amount. Its instant cast and +100% are its data; the charges come from the finisher proc. - Saber Fangs (984696): every third Swipe (Cat) hit on an enemy (counter 984697) applies Saber Fangs (984698: a bleed of your level plus 6% of your spell power and attack power every 3 sec), unless it already has yours (it can't be refreshed). Ferocious Bite deals 50% of its damage (984701) as Saber Thrash (984700) to up to 5 other enemies within 8 yards that have your Saber Fangs. - Bestial Assault (272317): each Hunter ability you cast that deals direct damage (not Auto Shot) adds a stack of 272319; at 5 the stacks go and a charge of Unrelenting Wrath comes back. Its own data procs are switched off (051's SQL). - Eternity (276362): an Align, Synchronize or Time-bind cast that leaves you at 0 energy gives Eternity (276347). - Arngrim's Greeting (997188): landing a Heroic Leap (its damage spell 86359) starts its Blizzard (997194: 215 Frost damage every 2 sec for 6 sec, its data) where you land and Icy Veins (997193: +20% casting speed, 6 sec). - Void Teleportation (954626): your (Priest) Void Shift also swaps your position with the target's, as they were when you cast it; skipped if either of you is on a transport, in a vehicle or on a flight path. - Overflowing Cultivation (273422): a Chain Heal cast with Overflowing (273420 from Flowing, or 1571973 from Overflow 1571951; Overflow 977710's own Overflowing 986113 already does this) up also puts Living Seed (48504) on each ally it heals, for 30% of the heal (its own value). - Shadow Word: Gore (983020): teaches Shadow Word: Gore (983021, wcme_enchant_teach); its damage is 8% higher for each other bleed of yours on the target when cast, up to 40%, for its whole duration. - Searing Flames (1180640): your Immolate can only be on one target; a new one takes the last one off. Your pet's Firebolt crit uses up its crit stacks (1180644). - Ol' Bloodsurge (983600): Mongoose Bite, Counterattack, Glaive Toss, Mortal Strike, Execute, Raging Blow, Whirlwind and Bloodthirst hits give Bloodsurge (983604: next Slam instant, free and ignoring armour, its data), at most once every 4 sec. - Lingering Death (81506): Shadow Word: Death's damage is also taken off the next heals the target receives within 3 sec, and for 6 sec enemies within 7 yards of where it hit are slowed by 40% (Earthbind 3600, renewed every sec). - Party Recuperation (983475): Party Recuperate (983478, taught) needs a stack of Bubbling Tankard (983476) and uses one. - Void Teleportation, Eternity and the rest above are cast-time rules; damage and heal rules are on the unit side.
- 052-epics-6.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch67_spells / wcme_epic_batch67_hooks): Double Agent (81439), Physics in Practice (277460), Saber Fangs (984696), Eternity (276362), Ursoc's Blessing (81446, 282004), Necromancer's Touch (275963), Arngrim's Greeting (997188), Void Teleportation (954626), Overflowing Cultivation (273422), Consumed By Shadows (997217).

**Assumptions:** Landing is Heroic Leap's damage spell (86359), which arrives with the leap's missile (speed 30); Blizzard is placed where the caster stands, so the script waits (checking every 0.1 sec, up to 2 sec) until you are on the ground within 4 yards of the landing point. Blizzard is its helper 997194 (215 Frost damage every 2 sec for 6 sec, radius index 8); its text formula ($m2*8 = 520) doesn't match the data (3 x 215 = 645). Icy Veins is 997193 (+20% casting speed, 6 sec). Blizzard and Icy Veins aren't checked as known. Review 2026-11-17: the first version cast Blizzard at the warrior's position as the damage spell went off, which can be mid-air.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Tooltip differs from what the server does (review):** Blizzard's text formula (520) doesn't match its data (645 over 6 sec).

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

### Blessed by the Elune (272253, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 09c12c0 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batch 5, spell side: - Final Shield (81255 / 281638): Power Word: Shield on an ally (you included) below 20% health removes the harmful crowd control on them (stun, fear, charm, polymorph, sleep, root, silence and the like); at most once every 30 sec, counted only when something was removed. - Blessed by the Elune (272253): after a Solar Eclipse your next Wrath or Starsurge also applies your highest known Insect Swarm; after a Lunar Eclipse your next Starfire or Starsurge also applies your highest known Moonfire. Each waits up to 30 sec. - War Mage (281939): Blink casts your highest known Arcanist's Explosion or Arcane Explosion where you land; each enemy in its radius takes 1.5 sec off Blink, up to 6 (9 sec), matching its helper 281943 / 281941. - Elemental Lord (276578): Elemental Blast or Lava Burst gives one of Elemental Earth (276579), Frost (276580) or Fire (276581) at random, replacing the last; each makes its shock deal 100% more direct damage (their data) and is used up by that shock: Earth Shock: Elemental Aftershock (276585) on up to 3 other enemies within 8 yards, 15% of the hit over 3 sec. Frost Shock: Elemental Solidification (276586): the next Lava Burst +55% (+20% against players). Flame Shock: Lava Burst's cooldown finishes, and for 12 sec (276587) each Flame Shock tick has a 20% chance to finish it again. - Spellstorm (271709), the chance part: with Arcane Evoked (271710), your next Frost damage spell that chills has an extra 80% chance to give Brain Freeze (57761) if you know Brain Freeze; with Fire Evoked (271713), your next Arcane Blast, Arcane Barrage, Fireball, Frostbolt or Frostfire Bolt has an extra 80% chance to give Missile Barrage (44401) if you know Missile Barrage. Either way the Evoked buff is used up. - Suffocating Roots (84378): when your Entangling Roots is dispelled, the dispeller gets the same roots from you.
- Epic batch 5, unit side: - Blessed by the Elune (272253): notes the Eclipse for the spell side. - Deter and Punish (977988 / 283979): while Deterrence lasts you also reflect spells (Spell Reflection 11818 set to 100%) and counterattack melee attacks from the front (Retaliation 20230, up to its 20 counterattacks); both end with Deterrence. - Spellstorm (271709): Missile Barrage used by Arcane Missiles gives Arcane Evoked (271710); Brain Freeze (57761) used by Fireball or Frostfire Bolt gives Fire Evoked (271713). Given just after, so the spell that used the proc doesn't use the new buff. - Predatory Nature (979050): each Predator's Swiftness you use restores 10 energy and adds a stack of 979051 (up to 2, 12 sec, its own data). Using it with 2 stacks instead uses them up for 979055: 5 combo points on your target (2 if the target is a player) and a 100% critical strike chance on the next Predator's Swiftness spell, which is removed once that spell is cast. - Suffocating Roots (84378): notes a dispelled Entangling Roots of yours for the spell side. - Soul Siphoner (81398): Haunt heals you for half, and heals the 3 lowest-health members of your party or raid within 40 yards for that half too. - Blood Shield (288804): in Frost Presence, Death Strike's heal also shields you (288805) for 100% of it, adding to what is left of the shield, capped at 20% of your maximum health (10% when your target is a player). - Tidecaller's Boon (271813): Healing Wave heals 50% more, less 4% for each stack of your Tidecaller's Boon (271814) on the target, then adds 3 stacks (up to 15, 10 sec). Each of your periodic heals on the target takes a stack off. - Twilight (84471): with Vampiric Embrace up, your bleeds heal through it like Shadow damage (you for its percentage of the tick, your party for a fifth of that), as the core's Vampiric Embrace does. - Elemental Lord (276578): Lava Burst +55% (+20% against players) with Elemental Solidification, which it uses up; Flame Shock ticks with Elemental Lava (276587) have a 20% chance to finish Lava Burst's cooldown.
- 051-epics-5.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch5_spells / wcme_epic_batch5_hooks): Final Shield (81255, 281638), Blessed by the Elune (272253), War Mage (281939), Elemental Lord (276578), Spellstorm (271709), Suffocating Roots (84378), Deter and Punish (977988, 283979), Predatory Nature (979050), Soul Siphoner (81398), Blood Shield (288804), Tidecaller's Boon (271813), Twilight (84471).

**Assumptions:** Each Eclipse arms its effect for up to 30 sec (no number given). Uses your highest known Insect Swarm or Moonfire, or rank 1 if you know none; Moonfire comes with its own small direct hit. A Starsurge with both armed applies both.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Tooltip differs from what the server does (review):** Moonfire also deals its direct damage, which the tooltip doesn't mention.

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batch 1 hooks: - Flash of Adrenaline (84453 / 282309): when Adrenaline Rush expires, Sprint's cooldown is finished. - Blinding Dispersion (283428 / 954634): when Dispersion ends, a shadow nova disorients enemies around you for 2 sec (283429 / 954635, their own area and duration). - Do The Stance Dance (81216): switching stances adds 6 sec to your Bloodrage, if it is running. - Spectre (81186): your periodic damage deals 10% more while you are stealthed. - Pestilent Opportunist (81188): your diseases deal 5% more to targets with one of your bleeds. - Down For The Count: 81263, Killing Spree deals 10% more to a target with a bleed or poison (anyone's); 281649, Killing Spree hits on such a target strike again for 25% of the damage (15% against players). - Duck Hunting (81110): your damage is 3% higher when the target is more than 5 yards above you, and your damaging spells remove Slow Fall from the target. - Freedom by Death (954128 / 283360): taking damage from your own Shadow Word: Death while stunned, feared, disoriented, rooted, silenced or pacified gives 954129 / 283361 (+20% damage and healing, 6 sec). Its cooldown cut is its own data. - Group Think (81453 / 282009 / 1581626): your pet (or charmed or tamed minion) deals 20% more while you have Berserk. - Elemental Torrent (1180420): Waterbolt damage from your Water Elemental gives you a stack of 1180423 (spell haste).
- 047-epics-1.sql: Most of these are hooks in wcme_epic_batch1_hooks and need no rows here: Flash of Adrenaline (84453, 282309), Blinding Dispersion (283428, 954634), Do The Stance Dance (81216), Spectre (81186), Pestilent Opportunist (81188), Down For The Count (81263, 281649), Duck Hunting (81110), Freedom by Death (954128, 283360), Group Think (81453, 282009, 1581626), Elemental Torrent (1180420).

**Assumptions:** Also fires when Dispersion is cancelled early ("ends"). Nova uses its own spell 283429 (area and 2 sec duration from the data). The cooldown cut was already its data.

Its cooldown cut is its own data. - Group Think (81453 / 282009 / 1581626): your pet (or charmed or tamed minion) deals 20% more while you have Berserk. - Elemental Torrent (1180420): Waterbolt damage from your Water Elemental gives you a stack of 1180423 (spell haste).
- 047-epics-1.sql: Most of these are hooks in wcme_epic_batch1_hooks and need no rows here: Flash of Adrenaline (84453, 282309), Blinding Dispersion (283428, 954634), Do The Stance Dance (81216), Spectre (81186), Pestilent Opportunist (81188), Down For The Count (81263, 281649), Duck Hunting (81110), Freedom by Death (954128, 283360), Group Think (81453, 282009, 1581626), Elemental Torrent (1180420).

**Assumptions:** As 283428, with its own nova 954635.

**Sources:** Ascension builder tooltip (area-52 archive, 14 Jun 2026); our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

### Blood Drinker (965389, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 75e2c05 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Spell-side parts (casts and critical strike chance): - Moon's Wrath (1180590): Wrath and Starsurge +8% crit chance on targets with your Moonfire. - Critical Time (1575124): your spells +5% crit chance on targets with your Living Bomb. - Unstoppable Force (911167): Slam always crits on an incapacitated or immobilized target. - Pyroclasmic Shadows (1577402): while your Pyroclasm buff is up, Shadow Bolt +20% crit chance (1577382's value). - Shooting Stars (81181): while your Starfall is up, +5% spell crit chance, and every non-channelled Arcane spell you cast adds half its cast time to Starfall. - Swashbuckler: Shocktrooper (81415): Shaman Shocks cast while Swashbuckling come back 10.5 sec sooner. - Esoteric Power (1556015): Arcane Blast's buff stacks to 5 (one more than normal). - Raising Hell (81096 / 281336): when you start Hellfire, your summoned demon (not an Infernal) casts it too; it stops when yours ends (wcme_epic_batch3_hooks). - Totem Master: Fire. 81445: your Fire totem gets health equal to 15% of yours (its mana cost and Searing / Magma cooldown changes are its data). 1581586: your Fire totem also gives Totem of Wrath's, or else Flametongue Totem's, aura if you know that totem (the aura comes from that totem's creature). - Blood Drinker (965389): Bloodthirst spends your Taste for Blood to heal you for 3% of your maximum health plus 25% of your attack power (no amount in the data; based on Bloodthirst's own small heal). - Deadly Follow-up (979080): damaging finishing moves give a charge of Poisoned Shiv (979082, up to 3), 10% per combo point (in spell_wcme_finisher_combo).
- 049-epics-3.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch3_spells / wcme_epic_batch3_hooks): Moon's Wrath (1180590), Critical Time (1575124), Unstoppable Force (911167), Pyroclasmic Shadows (1577402), Shooting Stars (81181), Swashbuckler: Shocktrooper (81415), Esoteric Power (1556015), Raising Hell (81096, 281336), Totem Master: Fire (81445, 1581586), Blood Drinker (965389), Sweeping Swings (912326), High Winds (954126), Timed Charge (954636), Fire and Ice (84382), Eclipse Bomber (275014).

**Assumptions:** Heal of 3% maximum health plus 25% attack power, chosen because the data has no amount and the text says small and attack-power based (Bloodthirst's own heal is a small percentage of health). Spends the whole Taste for Blood buff.

**Sources:** Ascension builder tooltip (area-52 archive, 14 Jun 2026); our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

### Blood Shield (288804, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 09c12c0 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batch 5, unit side: - Blessed by the Elune (272253): notes the Eclipse for the spell side. - Deter and Punish (977988 / 283979): while Deterrence lasts you also reflect spells (Spell Reflection 11818 set to 100%) and counterattack melee attacks from the front (Retaliation 20230, up to its 20 counterattacks); both end with Deterrence. - Spellstorm (271709): Missile Barrage used by Arcane Missiles gives Arcane Evoked (271710); Brain Freeze (57761) used by Fireball or Frostfire Bolt gives Fire Evoked (271713). Given just after, so the spell that used the proc doesn't use the new buff. - Predatory Nature (979050): each Predator's Swiftness you use restores 10 energy and adds a stack of 979051 (up to 2, 12 sec, its own data). Using it with 2 stacks instead uses them up for 979055: 5 combo points on your target (2 if the target is a player) and a 100% critical strike chance on the next Predator's Swiftness spell, which is removed once that spell is cast. - Suffocating Roots (84378): notes a dispelled Entangling Roots of yours for the spell side. - Soul Siphoner (81398): Haunt heals you for half, and heals the 3 lowest-health members of your party or raid within 40 yards for that half too. - Blood Shield (288804): in Frost Presence, Death Strike's heal also shields you (288805) for 100% of it, adding to what is left of the shield, capped at 20% of your maximum health (10% when your target is a player). - Tidecaller's Boon (271813): Healing Wave heals 50% more, less 4% for each stack of your Tidecaller's Boon (271814) on the target, then adds 3 stacks (up to 15, 10 sec). Each of your periodic heals on the target takes a stack off. - Twilight (84471): with Vampiric Embrace up, your bleeds heal through it like Shadow damage (you for its percentage of the tick, your party for a fifth of that), as the core's Vampiric Embrace does. - Elemental Lord (276578): Lava Burst +55% (+20% against players) with Elemental Solidification, which it uses up; Flame Shock ticks with Elemental Lava (276587) have a 20% chance to finish Lava Burst's cooldown.
- 051-epics-5.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch5_spells / wcme_epic_batch5_hooks): Final Shield (81255, 281638), Blessed by the Elune (272253), War Mage (281939), Elemental Lord (276578), Spellstorm (271709), Suffocating Roots (84378), Deter and Punish (977988, 283979), Predatory Nature (979050), Soul Siphoner (81398), Blood Shield (288804), Tidecaller's Boon (271813), Twilight (84471).

**Assumptions:** The tooltip's caps were missing from the data: 20% of your maximum health, 10% when your target is a player. 100% of the Death Strike heal before overhealing. Needs Frost Presence.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Tooltip differs from what the server does (review):** The shield caps (20% of maximum health, 10% against players) are our numbers; the tooltip's are blank in the data.

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

- Epic batch 4, unit side: - Pyroclasmic Ignition (271125): Explosive Shot +30% (20% against players) on a target with your Living Bomb. - Blood-Soaked Mutilation (1590974): Mutilate +6% per bleed of yours on the target; the out-of-Stealth Garrote's silence is removed. - Growing Light (270661): your Holy heals on your Beacon of Light target also heal 3 allies within 8 yards of it for 20% of the heal. - Holy Ground (81315 / 281788): your direct heals on an ally inside your Consecration also heal 5 allies within 8 yards for 5%.

**Assumptions:** The out-of-Stealth Garrote is allowed once within 10 sec of the Crimson Tempest proc (10 sec is our choice); its silence is removed right after it lands. "Your bleeds" are bleeds you applied.

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Finishing-move procs, 20% chance per combo point spent (10% for none here), checked at the cast while the combo points are still on you: - Cobra Combo (979106 / 284148): your pet gets 979105 (+15% critical strike chance). - Double Team (275094): 275096 on you (your pet's special attacks hit harder; its own data). - Deadly Combo (977856): needs Sudden Death; triggers Sudden Death (52437) and Deadly Combo (954353) through 977858. 283833: Sudden Death only (283835), and needs Sudden Death unless you have 973827. - Enraging Finale (283405): Bloodrage, if you know it (always with 973827), at most once every 20 sec and not while Bloodrage is up. - Titanic Finale (954436): the same Bloodrage proc from Rogue finishers while you can use Titan's Grip. - Adrenaline Junkie (1589466): 12 sec off Adrenaline Rush (its 1589467 value). - Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110): damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give a stack of Bloodsail Swashbuckling (983112, up to 10, 18 sec, the duration is not refreshed by new stacks) and the same number of stacks of 983113 (each main-hand auto attack gives 1 rage per stack, spell_wcme_bloodsail_rage). 285111: damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give 285115 (crit, up to 5). - Poison Bomb (979113): Envenom and Eviscerate take 4 sec off Poison Bomb (the text's 4 sec; its helper 979165 says 2). - Deadly Follow-up (979080): damaging finishers, 10% per combo point (the builder's number), give a charge of Poisoned Shiv (979082, up to 3), which Deadly Follow-up (979081) needs and spends. - Trauma Care (274205): Trauma Care (274211: next Greater Heal instant and +100%, its data), holding up to 2 charges; a new one starts with 1. - Party Recuperation (983475): 10% per combo point, a stack of Bubbling Tankard (983476, up to 4).
- 048-epics-2.sql: Finishing-move procs (spell_wcme_finisher_combo, 20% per combo point): Cobra Combo (979106, 284148), Double Team (275094), Deadly Combo (977856, 283833), Enraging Finale (283405), Titanic Finale (954436), Adrenaline Junkie (1589466), Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110, 285111; 983113 gives its rage on main-hand hits).

**Assumptions:** As 979106; the newer text says Tamed Pet of any kind, done as your active pet.

### Consumed By Shadows (997217, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 44ee48c | not merged yet | none yet | - |
| 2026-11-17 | 5db193a | not merged yet | none yet | - |
| 2026-11-17 | f70bb96 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batches 6 and 7, unit side: - Double Agent (81439): Ambush deals double damage to a target that was stealthed when the Ambush started (the core takes the target's stealth off before the damage is worked out). Its stealth detection is its data. - Physics in Practice (277460): Deadly Throw and Shuriken Toss deal more damage by your active Sprint's speed bonus (15% of it against players). - Shadow Word: Gore (983020): its ticks get the bonus worked out when it was cast. - Energy Surge (1180750): your damaging Rogue ability that hits a target with your Gouge deals 30% more (its own value); the Gouge then breaks as usual. - Ol' Bloodsurge (983600): using Bloodsurge makes your next Hunter or Warrior direct damage ability deal 20% more (within 15 sec). - Consumed By Shadows (997217): with Master of Subtlety (31665) up, your Shadow damage gets its bonus too (its value, 12% at rank 3); leaving Stealth puts you in Shadowform if you know it, unless you left Stealth by shifting into another form, are mounted, or are stealthed again. - Necromancer's Touch (275963): melee and spell hits from your Dominate Undead or Enslave Demon pet, or your ghoul, add a stack of Necromancer's Touch (275966) and of its Grave Coil part (288824) from you on the target (see NecromancerPet). Your next Grave Coil on the target uses up the Grave Coil stacks. - Searing Flames (1180640): each Immolate tick also hits every other enemy within 8 yards of the target for the same damage (1180643), and adds a stack of 1180644 (+3% spell crit) to your pet. - Lingering Death (81506): heals on the target take off what Shadow Word: Death dealt, until used up or 3 sec pass. - Downpour (983523): teaches Downpour (983524, wcme_enchant_teach). Each injured ally it heals gives you a stack of Torrential Riptide (983519, up to 5); when Riptide uses them, Downpour's cooldown drops 3 sec per stack. - Photosynthesis (287852): while your Lifebloom is on an ally (not you), each of your Nature heal-over-time ticks on them has a 20% chance to make it bloom (33778, worked out as the core's Lifebloom does) without using it up. - Vampirism (277003): Vampiric Embrace no longer heals you or your party; the part it would have healed you for goes instead to the lowest-health ally within 40 yards (you included), with your healing power added, up to 5% of your maximum health per heal. - Ursoc's Blessing (81446 / 282004): leaving Bear or Dire Bear Form keeps the armour and Stamina parts of the form's passive (Bear Form (Passive) / Dire Bear Form (Passive), effects 0 and 1, not its attack power) on you for 3 more sec. - Shadow Funnel (1180900): while you channel Health Funnel, each melee hit from your Voidwalker sets off Void Nova (1180902) around it. The no-threat part is its data. - Aegis Overdrive (299160): Mana Shield going up sets off Aegis Explosion (299163) unless its activation lockout (299161) is on you; Mana Shield running out or breaking sets it off again. - Trauma Care (274205) / Party Recuperation (983475): finisher procs in spell_wcme_finisher_combo.
- 052-epics-6.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch67_spells / wcme_epic_batch67_hooks): Double Agent (81439), Physics in Practice (277460), Saber Fangs (984696), Eternity (276362), Ursoc's Blessing (81446, 282004), Necromancer's Touch (275963), Arngrim's Greeting (997188), Void Teleportation (954626), Overflowing Cultivation (273422), Consumed By Shadows (997217).

**Assumptions:** Shadow damage (direct and over time) gets Master of Subtlety's bonus while its damage buff is up (during Stealth and the 6 sec after; 31665 or the twin the core picks for your talent rank; 12% at rank 3). Leaving Stealth casts your highest known Shadowform, but only if you end up in no form at all: not if Stealth ended because you shifted into cat or bear, not when mounted, and not if you are stealthed again. Rank 3 of Master of Subtlety is the enchant's requirement, not checked by the script. Review 2026-11-17: the first version only looked for 31665 itself (missing the talent twins) and would have forced Shadowform over a cat or bear form you shifted into from Stealth.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

- Unit-side parts: - Esoteric Power (1556015): each Arcane Blast hit has a 10% chance per Arcane Blast stack to deal 15% more as Esoteric Power (1556016). - Swashbuckler: Sweeping Swings (912326): while Sweeping Strikes is up you also have Critical Swings (912328, +2% melee crit); a melee ability hit takes 1 sec off Sweeping Strikes, at most once a second; with no other enemy within 8 yards, your hits also strike the primary target again for the same damage (Sweeping Strikes' own spell 26654), spending a Sweeping Strikes charge; Sweeping Strikes deals 25% less against players. - High Winds (954126): when your Cyclone ends on a target, it gets 954127 (-20% damage and healing, its own 4 sec). - Timed Charge (954636): when your Alter Time ends (you return), 954637 bursts around you (Arcane damage, 3 sec stun). - Fire and Ice (84382): your Deep Freeze leaves Frozen Flesh (944829, 3 charges) on the target; your Fire spells on it trigger Thawed Flesh (944831) and spend a charge. - Eclipse Bomber (275014): gaining Eclipse (Solar) gives Solar Bomber (274981) and ends your Lunar Flames; gaining Eclipse (Lunar) gives Lunar Bomber (274982) and ends your Solar Flames. With Solar Bomber, Fireball and Pyroblast hits give Lunar Flames (274983, Starfire / Starsurge damage); with Lunar Bomber, Starfire and Starsurge hits give Solar Flames (275002, Fireball / Pyroblast damage), as the enchant's text says. - Controlled Aggression (1181300): Shield Slam deals 25% of your attack power more; Devastate and Revenge get a second roll at your Sword and Board talent's chance (10% per rank). - Poison Bomb (979113): Envenom and Eviscerate have a 20% chance per combo point to take 4 sec off Poison Bomb (in spell_wcme_finisher_combo). - Raising Hell: your demon's Hellfire stops when yours does.

**Assumptions:** Its helper 1181301 (kept on you) supplies the longer Shield Slam cooldown. "Chance doubled" is done as a second roll at the talent's 10% per rank on Devastate and Revenge.

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Deadly Bite (272353): Mongoose Bite, Counterattack or Savage Blade damage puts 40% of that hit into a bleed over its duration; a new hit adds to what is left of the bleed and refreshes it.
- Deadly Bite (965292), Ascension's own version (the builder tooltip; our client links the same spells): teaches Savage Blade, which applies Deadly Bite itself. Mongoose Bite and Counterattack damage apply Deadly Bite (965408: a bleed of 66 + 3.4% SP + 3.4% ranged AP every 2 sec, +20% Raptor Strike and Savage Blade damage, up to 5 stacks, 12 sec). Deadly Bite's damage has a chance to trigger Sharpened Blade: 20% per tick (no chance survives; assumed), which takes 4 sec off Savage Blade's cooldown and 1 sec off Glaive Toss's, and stacks up the next Savage Blade's damage. (Its twin 272353 keeps the other design: a share of the hit, accumulated into one bleed.)
- 017-enchant-scripts.sql: Rush of Blood 1587050, Martial Crescendo 84752, Tools of War 84750, Curse of Arugal 982806, Primordial Fury 977717, Ice Lash 977764, Words of Healing 81470, Ambidextrous 977771, Nature's Fervor 965283, Controlled Burn 81407, Eldritch Knight 81116, Disciple of the Tides 274921, Twilight Paragon 977824, Consecrated Strikes 965295, Consecrated Weapon 977848, Titanic Mutilate 954067, Deadly Bite 272353, Hand of the Adorned 986003, Arcane Thunder Seer 997055, Arcane Thunder 997056, Divine Purpose 965421, Benevolent Crusader 81251, Unbounded Spell Slinger 1568060, Evoker 953308.
- 019-tooltip-review.sql: Deadly Bite's own version (965292) is Ascension's real design, not a placeholder for its twin 272353: it teaches Savage Blade and stacks its own bleed (spell_wcme_deadly_bite_stacks). 004 no longer blanks it or links it to 272353.

**What the pull requests said:**

- #164: Only the named abilities set it off** (`spell_wcme_enchant_action` rule): Dark Surgeon, Fire Watch, Midnight Flames, Wind Rager, Deadly Bite (both spells), Blood Tracker: Deadly Bite, Circle of Life, Adorned by Light, Lava Sweep (Doomhammer's Fury), Warcry (30 sec lockout for other shouts), Arcane Thunder Seer, Divine Purpose, Endless Agony (both), Evoker, Nature's Fervor (both), One With The Light, Tidecaller (Healing Wave crits), Titanic Mutilate, Transcendental Embrace, Unbounded Spell Slinger, Blooming Growth.
- #166: Deadly Bite | The bleed is 40% of the triggering hit, added to what's left of a running bleed; the enchant's placeholder copy (965292) loses the duplicate rule #164 gave it |
- #169: Deadly Bite (965292):** this is Ascension's real version, not a placeholder for its twin 272353. It teaches Savage Blade, and Mongoose Bite and Counterattack apply the stacking bleed (up to 5 stacks). Bleed ticks have a 20% chance of Sharpened Blade, which cuts Savage Blade's cooldown by 4 sec. The 20% is assumed; the tooltip only says "a chance". `004` no longer blanks it or links it to 272353.
- #169: Follow our client's own spell text:** Deadly Bite 272353, Warcry's 30 sec lockout, Frost Lich's 12 sec and Spell Slinger's 6 sec. The site doesn't mention these.

- Finishing-move procs, 20% chance per combo point spent (10% for none here), checked at the cast while the combo points are still on you: - Cobra Combo (979106 / 284148): your pet gets 979105 (+15% critical strike chance). - Double Team (275094): 275096 on you (your pet's special attacks hit harder; its own data). - Deadly Combo (977856): needs Sudden Death; triggers Sudden Death (52437) and Deadly Combo (954353) through 977858. 283833: Sudden Death only (283835), and needs Sudden Death unless you have 973827. - Enraging Finale (283405): Bloodrage, if you know it (always with 973827), at most once every 20 sec and not while Bloodrage is up. - Titanic Finale (954436): the same Bloodrage proc from Rogue finishers while you can use Titan's Grip. - Adrenaline Junkie (1589466): 12 sec off Adrenaline Rush (its 1589467 value). - Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110): damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give a stack of Bloodsail Swashbuckling (983112, up to 10, 18 sec, the duration is not refreshed by new stacks) and the same number of stacks of 983113 (each main-hand auto attack gives 1 rage per stack, spell_wcme_bloodsail_rage). 285111: damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give 285115 (crit, up to 5). - Poison Bomb (979113): Envenom and Eviscerate take 4 sec off Poison Bomb (the text's 4 sec; its helper 979165 says 2). - Deadly Follow-up (979080): damaging finishers, 10% per combo point (the builder's number), give a charge of Poisoned Shiv (979082, up to 3), which Deadly Follow-up (979081) needs and spends. - Trauma Care (274205): Trauma Care (274211: next Greater Heal instant and +100%, its data), holding up to 2 charges; a new one starts with 1. - Party Recuperation (983475): 10% per combo point, a stack of Bubbling Tankard (983476, up to 4).
- 048-epics-2.sql: Finishing-move procs (spell_wcme_finisher_combo, 20% per combo point): Cobra Combo (979106, 284148), Double Team (275094), Deadly Combo (977856, 283833), Enraging Finale (283405), Titanic Finale (954436), Adrenaline Junkie (1589466), Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110, 285111; 983113 gives its rage on main-hand hits).

**Assumptions:** Sudden Death only (283835); needs the talent unless you have 973827, as its text says.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

- Spell-side parts (casts and critical strike chance): - Moon's Wrath (1180590): Wrath and Starsurge +8% crit chance on targets with your Moonfire. - Critical Time (1575124): your spells +5% crit chance on targets with your Living Bomb. - Unstoppable Force (911167): Slam always crits on an incapacitated or immobilized target. - Pyroclasmic Shadows (1577402): while your Pyroclasm buff is up, Shadow Bolt +20% crit chance (1577382's value). - Shooting Stars (81181): while your Starfall is up, +5% spell crit chance, and every non-channelled Arcane spell you cast adds half its cast time to Starfall. - Swashbuckler: Shocktrooper (81415): Shaman Shocks cast while Swashbuckling come back 10.5 sec sooner. - Esoteric Power (1556015): Arcane Blast's buff stacks to 5 (one more than normal). - Raising Hell (81096 / 281336): when you start Hellfire, your summoned demon (not an Infernal) casts it too; it stops when yours ends (wcme_epic_batch3_hooks). - Totem Master: Fire. 81445: your Fire totem gets health equal to 15% of yours (its mana cost and Searing / Magma cooldown changes are its data). 1581586: your Fire totem also gives Totem of Wrath's, or else Flametongue Totem's, aura if you know that totem (the aura comes from that totem's creature). - Blood Drinker (965389): Bloodthirst spends your Taste for Blood to heal you for 3% of your maximum health plus 25% of your attack power (no amount in the data; based on Bloodthirst's own small heal). - Deadly Follow-up (979080): damaging finishing moves give a charge of Poisoned Shiv (979082, up to 3), 10% per combo point (in spell_wcme_finisher_combo).

**Assumptions:** Deadly Follow-up is taught while you know Shiv; charges last 979082's own duration and stop at 3; the dagger requirement is the ability's own.

### Demolisher (273786, Epic)

**Status:** changed, awaiting in-game test

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-31 | b1f1d81 | #20 Enchant fixes batch 3: 67 twin links plus matching tooltips | build6 (2026-10-31) | 2026-11-02 |
| 2026-11-16 | 63a06c9 | #175 Medium legendary fixes, batches 4 to 8 | build50 (2026-11-16) | 2026-11-17 |
| 2026-11-16 | 107f408 | #175 Medium legendary fixes, batches 4 to 8 | build50 (2026-11-16) | 2026-11-17 |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Demolisher (977864): its own design instead of twin 273786 (003 no longer links them). Mortal Strike shows as Demolishing Strike (947486, WcmeSyncSwap: the core does not swap it, the names differ), which puts Demolish on the target (273795: 2% more Physical damage taken from you per stack, 6 stacks). Each of your melee critical strikes on it adds a stack; at 6 stacks Demolish is spent and your next of these abilities is empowered: Expose Armor gives you Demolishing Strikes (954090: 35% armor penetration for 20 sec); Riposte awards 2 more combo points and slows the target's attack speed by 10% more; Mongoose Bite triggers no cooldown and deals as much as a critical strike with 100% more damage (50% against players).
- 029-medium-legendaries-6.sql: Demolisher (977864): its own design instead of twin 273786 (003 no longer links them).

**What the pull requests said:**

- #175: Demolisher (977864):** its own design instead of twin 273786 (link and addon tooltip twin removed). Demolishing Strike applies Demolish; melee crits stack it; at 6 stacks your next Expose Armor (35% armor penetration), Riposte (+2 combo points, 10% more attack speed slow) or Mongoose Bite (no cooldown, crit-level damage +100%, 50% vs players) is empowered.
- #175: Ability swaps (affects earlier batches too):** the core only swaps an enchant's ability when the new name contains the old one, or the description says "transform". A new helper (`WcmeSyncSwap`) now does the swap for Champion of Justice (Crusader Strike → Crusader's Justice, only while Righteous Justice is up), Demolisher (Mortal Strike → Demolishing Strike), Dragon Warrior (Death Wish → Dragon Roar, Intercept → Dragon Charge), Astral Bomber, Cauterizing Flames and Dark Ranger. Fel Corruption now teaches Felblade alongside Bloodthirst.

### Deter and Punish (283979, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 09c12c0 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batch 5, unit side: - Blessed by the Elune (272253): notes the Eclipse for the spell side. - Deter and Punish (977988 / 283979): while Deterrence lasts you also reflect spells (Spell Reflection 11818 set to 100%) and counterattack melee attacks from the front (Retaliation 20230, up to its 20 counterattacks); both end with Deterrence. - Spellstorm (271709): Missile Barrage used by Arcane Missiles gives Arcane Evoked (271710); Brain Freeze (57761) used by Fireball or Frostfire Bolt gives Fire Evoked (271713). Given just after, so the spell that used the proc doesn't use the new buff. - Predatory Nature (979050): each Predator's Swiftness you use restores 10 energy and adds a stack of 979051 (up to 2, 12 sec, its own data). Using it with 2 stacks instead uses them up for 979055: 5 combo points on your target (2 if the target is a player) and a 100% critical strike chance on the next Predator's Swiftness spell, which is removed once that spell is cast. - Suffocating Roots (84378): notes a dispelled Entangling Roots of yours for the spell side. - Soul Siphoner (81398): Haunt heals you for half, and heals the 3 lowest-health members of your party or raid within 40 yards for that half too. - Blood Shield (288804): in Frost Presence, Death Strike's heal also shields you (288805) for 100% of it, adding to what is left of the shield, capped at 20% of your maximum health (10% when your target is a player). - Tidecaller's Boon (271813): Healing Wave heals 50% more, less 4% for each stack of your Tidecaller's Boon (271814) on the target, then adds 3 stacks (up to 15, 10 sec). Each of your periodic heals on the target takes a stack off. - Twilight (84471): with Vampiric Embrace up, your bleeds heal through it like Shadow damage (you for its percentage of the tick, your party for a fifth of that), as the core's Vampiric Embrace does. - Elemental Lord (276578): Lava Burst +55% (+20% against players) with Elemental Solidification, which it uses up; Flame Shock ticks with Elemental Lava (276587) have a 20% chance to finish Lava Burst's cooldown.
- 051-epics-5.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch5_spells / wcme_epic_batch5_hooks): Final Shield (81255, 281638), Blessed by the Elune (272253), War Mage (281939), Elemental Lord (276578), Spellstorm (271709), Suffocating Roots (84378), Deter and Punish (977988, 283979), Predatory Nature (979050), Soul Siphoner (81398), Blood Shield (288804), Tidecaller's Boon (271813), Twilight (84471).

**Assumptions:** As 977988.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 4a0748b | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batch 1 hooks: - Flash of Adrenaline (84453 / 282309): when Adrenaline Rush expires, Sprint's cooldown is finished. - Blinding Dispersion (283428 / 954634): when Dispersion ends, a shadow nova disorients enemies around you for 2 sec (283429 / 954635, their own area and duration). - Do The Stance Dance (81216): switching stances adds 6 sec to your Bloodrage, if it is running. - Spectre (81186): your periodic damage deals 10% more while you are stealthed. - Pestilent Opportunist (81188): your diseases deal 5% more to targets with one of your bleeds. - Down For The Count: 81263, Killing Spree deals 10% more to a target with a bleed or poison (anyone's); 281649, Killing Spree hits on such a target strike again for 25% of the damage (15% against players). - Duck Hunting (81110): your damage is 3% higher when the target is more than 5 yards above you, and your damaging spells remove Slow Fall from the target. - Freedom by Death (954128 / 283360): taking damage from your own Shadow Word: Death while stunned, feared, disoriented, rooted, silenced or pacified gives 954129 / 283361 (+20% damage and healing, 6 sec). Its cooldown cut is its own data. - Group Think (81453 / 282009 / 1581626): your pet (or charmed or tamed minion) deals 20% more while you have Berserk. - Elemental Torrent (1180420): Waterbolt damage from your Water Elemental gives you a stack of 1180423 (spell haste).
- 047-epics-1.sql: Most of these are hooks in wcme_epic_batch1_hooks and need no rows here: Flash of Adrenaline (84453, 282309), Blinding Dispersion (283428, 954634), Do The Stance Dance (81216), Spectre (81186), Pestilent Opportunist (81188), Down For The Count (81263, 281649), Duck Hunting (81110), Freedom by Death (954128, 283360), Group Think (81453, 282009, 1581626), Elemental Torrent (1180420).

**Assumptions:** No cap on how often stance switches extend Bloodrage, as written.

**Sources:** Ascension builder tooltip (area-52 archive, 14 Jun 2026); our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

### Double Agent (81439, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 44ee48c | not merged yet | none yet | - |
| 2026-11-17 | 3c1c790 | not merged yet | none yet | - |
| 2026-11-17 | f70bb96 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batches 6 and 7, unit side: - Double Agent (81439): Ambush deals double damage to a target that was stealthed when the Ambush started (the core takes the target's stealth off before the damage is worked out). Its stealth detection is its data. - Physics in Practice (277460): Deadly Throw and Shuriken Toss deal more damage by your active Sprint's speed bonus (15% of it against players). - Shadow Word: Gore (983020): its ticks get the bonus worked out when it was cast. - Energy Surge (1180750): your damaging Rogue ability that hits a target with your Gouge deals 30% more (its own value); the Gouge then breaks as usual. - Ol' Bloodsurge (983600): using Bloodsurge makes your next Hunter or Warrior direct damage ability deal 20% more (within 15 sec). - Consumed By Shadows (997217): with Master of Subtlety (31665) up, your Shadow damage gets its bonus too (its value, 12% at rank 3); leaving Stealth puts you in Shadowform if you know it, unless you left Stealth by shifting into another form, are mounted, or are stealthed again. - Necromancer's Touch (275963): melee and spell hits from your Dominate Undead or Enslave Demon pet, or your ghoul, add a stack of Necromancer's Touch (275966) and of its Grave Coil part (288824) from you on the target (see NecromancerPet). Your next Grave Coil on the target uses up the Grave Coil stacks. - Searing Flames (1180640): each Immolate tick also hits every other enemy within 8 yards of the target for the same damage (1180643), and adds a stack of 1180644 (+3% spell crit) to your pet. - Lingering Death (81506): heals on the target take off what Shadow Word: Death dealt, until used up or 3 sec pass. - Downpour (983523): teaches Downpour (983524, wcme_enchant_teach). Each injured ally it heals gives you a stack of Torrential Riptide (983519, up to 5); when Riptide uses them, Downpour's cooldown drops 3 sec per stack. - Photosynthesis (287852): while your Lifebloom is on an ally (not you), each of your Nature heal-over-time ticks on them has a 20% chance to make it bloom (33778, worked out as the core's Lifebloom does) without using it up. - Vampirism (277003): Vampiric Embrace no longer heals you or your party; the part it would have healed you for goes instead to the lowest-health ally within 40 yards (you included), with your healing power added, up to 5% of your maximum health per heal. - Ursoc's Blessing (81446 / 282004): leaving Bear or Dire Bear Form keeps the armour and Stamina parts of the form's passive (Bear Form (Passive) / Dire Bear Form (Passive), effects 0 and 1, not its attack power) on you for 3 more sec. - Shadow Funnel (1180900): while you channel Health Funnel, each melee hit from your Voidwalker sets off Void Nova (1180902) around it. The no-threat part is its data. - Aegis Overdrive (299160): Mana Shield going up sets off Aegis Explosion (299163) unless its activation lockout (299161) is on you; Mana Shield running out or breaking sets it off again. - Trauma Care (274205) / Party Recuperation (983475): finisher procs in spell_wcme_finisher_combo.
- 052-epics-6.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch67_spells / wcme_epic_batch67_hooks): Double Agent (81439), Physics in Practice (277460), Saber Fangs (984696), Eternity (276362), Ursoc's Blessing (81446, 282004), Necromancer's Touch (275963), Arngrim's Greeting (997188), Void Teleportation (954626), Overflowing Cultivation (273422), Consumed By Shadows (997217).

**Assumptions:** Stealthed means the target had a stealth aura when the Ambush started. Review 2026-11-17: the first version checked when the damage was worked out, by which time the core has already taken the target's stealth off, so it never fired. The stealth detection is its data.

**Sources:** Ascension builder tooltip (area-52 archive, 14 Jun 2026); our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Testing:** nothing recorded yet

### Double Team (275094, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 07911c5 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Finishing-move procs, 20% chance per combo point spent (10% for none here), checked at the cast while the combo points are still on you: - Cobra Combo (979106 / 284148): your pet gets 979105 (+15% critical strike chance). - Double Team (275094): 275096 on you (your pet's special attacks hit harder; its own data). - Deadly Combo (977856): needs Sudden Death; triggers Sudden Death (52437) and Deadly Combo (954353) through 977858. 283833: Sudden Death only (283835), and needs Sudden Death unless you have 973827. - Enraging Finale (283405): Bloodrage, if you know it (always with 973827), at most once every 20 sec and not while Bloodrage is up. - Titanic Finale (954436): the same Bloodrage proc from Rogue finishers while you can use Titan's Grip. - Adrenaline Junkie (1589466): 12 sec off Adrenaline Rush (its 1589467 value). - Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110): damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give a stack of Bloodsail Swashbuckling (983112, up to 10, 18 sec, the duration is not refreshed by new stacks) and the same number of stacks of 983113 (each main-hand auto attack gives 1 rage per stack, spell_wcme_bloodsail_rage). 285111: damaging finishers while Swashbuckling give 285115 (crit, up to 5). - Poison Bomb (979113): Envenom and Eviscerate take 4 sec off Poison Bomb (the text's 4 sec; its helper 979165 says 2). - Deadly Follow-up (979080): damaging finishers, 10% per combo point (the builder's number), give a charge of Poisoned Shiv (979082, up to 3), which Deadly Follow-up (979081) needs and spends. - Trauma Care (274205): Trauma Care (274211: next Greater Heal instant and +100%, its data), holding up to 2 charges; a new one starts with 1. - Party Recuperation (983475): 10% per combo point, a stack of Bubbling Tankard (983476, up to 4).
- 048-epics-2.sql: Finishing-move procs (spell_wcme_finisher_combo, 20% per combo point): Cobra Combo (979106, 284148), Double Team (275094), Deadly Combo (977856, 283833), Enraging Finale (283405), Titanic Finale (954436), Adrenaline Junkie (1589466), Swashbuckler: Bloodsail (983110, 285111; 983113 gives its rage on main-hand hits).

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

### Dragon Warrior (271907, Epic)

**Status:** changed, awaiting in-game test

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-31 | 360dc22 | #23 Enchant fixes batch 4: 38 twins without double bonuses | build6 (2026-10-31) | 2026-11-02 |
| 2026-11-16 | a224506 | #175 Medium legendary fixes, batches 4 to 8 | build50 (2026-11-16) | 2026-11-17 |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Dragon Warrior (954252): its own design instead of twin 271907 (004 no longer links them). Bloodthirst and Execute hits give Draconic Slam (954261: the next Slam is instant, the data's own spell mod); that Slam deals 50% more as extra damage and grants a stack of Dragon Fury (954253, up to 3, with its Dragon Fury Visual 954254): each stack takes 5 sec off Dragon Roar and makes it deal 50% more (the data's own mod). Death Wish shows as Dragon Roar (954250) and Intercept as Dragon Charge (954256) (WcmeSyncSwap: the core does not swap them, the names differ). Dragon Roar spends the stacks and stuns (954255) only with 3 of them (spell_wcme_dragon_roar_stun).
- 038-core-handlers-and-visuals.sql: Dragon Warrior (954252): its own design instead of twin 271907 (004 no longer overrides or links it): Draconic Slam, Dragon Fury stacks with their own visual, and the Dragon Roar stun only at 3 stacks.

**What the pull requests said:**

- #175: Dragon Warrior (954252):** its own design instead of twin 271907 (004 override and link removed, addon tooltip twin removed): Bloodthirst/Execute give Draconic Slam (instant Slam that deals 50% more and grants Dragon Fury); Dragon Fury stacks (up to 3) cut Dragon Roar's cooldown by 5 sec each and use their own visual; Dragon Roar's stun only happens at 3 stacks, and it spends them.
- #175: Ability swaps (affects earlier batches too):** the core only swaps an enchant's ability when the new name contains the old one, or the description says "transform". A new helper (`WcmeSyncSwap`) now does the swap for Champion of Justice (Crusader Strike → Crusader's Justice, only while Righteous Justice is up), Demolisher (Mortal Strike → Demolishing Strike), Dragon Warrior (Death Wish → Dragon Roar, Intercept → Dragon Charge), Astral Bomber, Cauterizing Flames and Dark Ranger. Fel Corruption now teaches Felblade alongside Bloodthirst.

With Solar Bomber, Fireball and Pyroblast hits give Lunar Flames (274983, Starfire / Starsurge damage); with Lunar Bomber, Starfire and Starsurge hits give Solar Flames (275002, Fireball / Pyroblast damage), as the enchant's text says. - Controlled Aggression (1181300): Shield Slam deals 25% of your attack power more; Devastate and Revenge get a second roll at your Sword and Board talent's chance (10% per rank). - Poison Bomb (979113): Envenom and Eviscerate have a 20% chance per combo point to take 4 sec off Poison Bomb (in spell_wcme_finisher_combo). - Raising Hell: your demon's Hellfire stops when yours does.

**Assumptions:** The Bomber buffs' data points at the opposite Flames spells from the text; we follow the text (Fireball/Pyroblast give Lunar Flames, Starfire/Starsurge give Solar Flames).

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batches 6 and 7, spell side. Not here because the core already does them (review 2026-11-17): Electrical Discharge (277082, hard-coded in AuraEffect::HandleAscensionPercentEventProc), Pious Strikes (1581647) and Holy Servitude (1584997) (both coa_mystic_enchant_proc rules run by spell_ascension_mystic_enchant_proc). - Trauma Care (274205): a Greater Heal cast with Trauma Care (274211) also heals the lowest-health injured ally within 40 yards for the same amount. Its instant cast and +100% are its data; the charges come from the finisher proc. - Saber Fangs (984696): every third Swipe (Cat) hit on an enemy (counter 984697) applies Saber Fangs (984698: a bleed of your level plus 6% of your spell power and attack power every 3 sec), unless it already has yours (it can't be refreshed). Ferocious Bite deals 50% of its damage (984701) as Saber Thrash (984700) to up to 5 other enemies within 8 yards that have your Saber Fangs. - Bestial Assault (272317): each Hunter ability you cast that deals direct damage (not Auto Shot) adds a stack of 272319; at 5 the stacks go and a charge of Unrelenting Wrath comes back. Its own data procs are switched off (051's SQL). - Eternity (276362): an Align, Synchronize or Time-bind cast that leaves you at 0 energy gives Eternity (276347). - Arngrim's Greeting (997188): landing a Heroic Leap (its damage spell 86359) starts its Blizzard (997194: 215 Frost damage every 2 sec for 6 sec, its data) where you land and Icy Veins (997193: +20% casting speed, 6 sec). - Void Teleportation (954626): your (Priest) Void Shift also swaps your position with the target's, as they were when you cast it; skipped if either of you is on a transport, in a vehicle or on a flight path. - Overflowing Cultivation (273422): a Chain Heal cast with Overflowing (273420 from Flowing, or 1571973 from Overflow 1571951; Overflow 977710's own Overflowing 986113 already does this) up also puts Living Seed (48504) on each ally it heals, for 30% of the heal (its own value). - Shadow Word: Gore (983020): teaches Shadow Word: Gore (983021, wcme_enchant_teach); its damage is 8% higher for each other bleed of yours on the target when cast, up to 40%, for its whole duration. - Searing Flames (1180640): your Immolate can only be on one target; a new one takes the last one off. Your pet's Firebolt crit uses up its crit stacks (1180644). - Ol' Bloodsurge (983600): Mongoose Bite, Counterattack, Glaive Toss, Mortal Strike, Execute, Raging Blow, Whirlwind and Bloodthirst hits give Bloodsurge (983604: next Slam instant, free and ignoring armour, its data), at most once every 4 sec. - Lingering Death (81506): Shadow Word: Death's damage is also taken off the next heals the target receives within 3 sec, and for 6 sec enemies within 7 yards of where it hit are slowed by 40% (Earthbind 3600, renewed every sec). - Party Recuperation (983475): Party Recuperate (983478, taught) needs a stack of Bubbling Tankard (983476) and uses one. - Void Teleportation, Eternity and the rest above are cast-time rules; damage and heal rules are on the unit side.
- 052-epics-6.sql: Electrical Discharge (277082), Holy Servitude (1584997) and Pious Strikes (1581647) were dropped in review: the core already does them (no rows were ever added for them).

**Assumptions:** Review 2026-11-17: the core already does this enchant itself (hard-coded in AuraEffect::HandleAscensionPercentEventProc: on a Mangle (Bear) hit, 15% chance, it casts the helper 277083 with 20% of the hit, an area damage over time around you of 3 ticks, 60% in all). Our batch 6 script did it a second time and has been removed; nothing of ours acts on it now.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

### Elemental Lord (276578, Epic)

**Status:** changed on an unmerged branch

| Date | Commit | Pull request | First build with it | Installed on the server |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-17 | 09c12c0 | not merged yet | none yet | - |

**What the server does (from the code and SQL comments):**

- Epic batch 5, spell side: - Final Shield (81255 / 281638): Power Word: Shield on an ally (you included) below 20% health removes the harmful crowd control on them (stun, fear, charm, polymorph, sleep, root, silence and the like); at most once every 30 sec, counted only when something was removed. - Blessed by the Elune (272253): after a Solar Eclipse your next Wrath or Starsurge also applies your highest known Insect Swarm; after a Lunar Eclipse your next Starfire or Starsurge also applies your highest known Moonfire. Each waits up to 30 sec. - War Mage (281939): Blink casts your highest known Arcanist's Explosion or Arcane Explosion where you land; each enemy in its radius takes 1.5 sec off Blink, up to 6 (9 sec), matching its helper 281943 / 281941. - Elemental Lord (276578): Elemental Blast or Lava Burst gives one of Elemental Earth (276579), Frost (276580) or Fire (276581) at random, replacing the last; each makes its shock deal 100% more direct damage (their data) and is used up by that shock: Earth Shock: Elemental Aftershock (276585) on up to 3 other enemies within 8 yards, 15% of the hit over 3 sec. Frost Shock: Elemental Solidification (276586): the next Lava Burst +55% (+20% against players). Flame Shock: Lava Burst's cooldown finishes, and for 12 sec (276587) each Flame Shock tick has a 20% chance to finish it again. - Spellstorm (271709), the chance part: with Arcane Evoked (271710), your next Frost damage spell that chills has an extra 80% chance to give Brain Freeze (57761) if you know Brain Freeze; with Fire Evoked (271713), your next Arcane Blast, Arcane Barrage, Fireball, Frostbolt or Frostfire Bolt has an extra 80% chance to give Missile Barrage (44401) if you know Missile Barrage. Either way the Evoked buff is used up. - Suffocating Roots (84378): when your Entangling Roots is dispelled, the dispeller gets the same roots from you.
- Epic batch 5, unit side: - Blessed by the Elune (272253): notes the Eclipse for the spell side. - Deter and Punish (977988 / 283979): while Deterrence lasts you also reflect spells (Spell Reflection 11818 set to 100%) and counterattack melee attacks from the front (Retaliation 20230, up to its 20 counterattacks); both end with Deterrence. - Spellstorm (271709): Missile Barrage used by Arcane Missiles gives Arcane Evoked (271710); Brain Freeze (57761) used by Fireball or Frostfire Bolt gives Fire Evoked (271713). Given just after, so the spell that used the proc doesn't use the new buff. - Predatory Nature (979050): each Predator's Swiftness you use restores 10 energy and adds a stack of 979051 (up to 2, 12 sec, its own data). Using it with 2 stacks instead uses them up for 979055: 5 combo points on your target (2 if the target is a player) and a 100% critical strike chance on the next Predator's Swiftness spell, which is removed once that spell is cast. - Suffocating Roots (84378): notes a dispelled Entangling Roots of yours for the spell side. - Soul Siphoner (81398): Haunt heals you for half, and heals the 3 lowest-health members of your party or raid within 40 yards for that half too. - Blood Shield (288804): in Frost Presence, Death Strike's heal also shields you (288805) for 100% of it, adding to what is left of the shield, capped at 20% of your maximum health (10% when your target is a player). - Tidecaller's Boon (271813): Healing Wave heals 50% more, less 4% for each stack of your Tidecaller's Boon (271814) on the target, then adds 3 stacks (up to 15, 10 sec). Each of your periodic heals on the target takes a stack off. - Twilight (84471): with Vampiric Embrace up, your bleeds heal through it like Shadow damage (you for its percentage of the tick, your party for a fifth of that), as the core's Vampiric Embrace does. - Elemental Lord (276578): Lava Burst +55% (+20% against players) with Elemental Solidification, which it uses up; Flame Shock ticks with Elemental Lava (276587) have a 20% chance to finish Lava Burst's cooldown.
- 051-epics-5.sql: Hooks only (wcme_epic_batch5_spells / wcme_epic_batch5_hooks): Final Shield (81255, 281638), Blessed by the Elune (272253), War Mage (281939), Elemental Lord (276578), Spellstorm (271709), Suffocating Roots (84378), Deter and Punish (977988, 283979), Predatory Nature (979050), Soul Siphoner (81398), Blood Shield (288804), Tidecaller's Boon (271813), Twilight (84471).

**Assumptions:** Earth: 15% of the Earth Shock hit split over 3 ticks on up to 3 other enemies within 8 yards (its helper's 15). Frost: +55% next Lava Burst (+20% against players), used up by it. Fire: the empowered Flame Shock always finishes Lava Burst's cooldown (its helper's 100% chance) and its ticks for 12 sec have 20%. The empowerment from Elemental Blast or Lava Burst replaces the last one. The tethered-elemental bonuses in the helper texts are not done. Review 2026-11-17: Elemental Aftershock (276585) is set to skip your damage bonuses, because its amount is already 15% of a finished Earth Shock hit.

**Sources:** our client spell data (Spell.dbc)

**Decisions:** 2026-11-17 James: assumptions and new features are fine; base missing numbers on similar existing spells.

**Tooltip differs from what the server does (review):** The tethered-elemental parts of Elemental Aftershock and Elemental Lava are not done.
